Weir Cook Terminal contains two concourses and 40 gates.[^cite_note-479] In 2024, the airport served 10.5 million passengers and offered nonstop service to 53 domestic and international destinations.[^cite_note-480] As home to the second largest [FedEx Express](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FedEx_Express) hub in the world, it ranks among the [ten busiest U.S. airports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_the_busiest_airports_in_the_United_States#10_busiest_airports_in_the_U.S._by_total_cargo_throughput_\(2017\)) in terms of air cargo throughput.[^cite_note-481] The [Indianapolis Airport Authority](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indianapolis_Airport_Authority) oversees operations at five additional aviation facilities in the region, two of which are located in the city: [Eagle Creek Airpark](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eagle_Creek_Airpark) and the [Indianapolis Downtown Heliport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indianapolis_Downtown_Heliport).[^cite_note-482]

[Indianapolis Union Station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indianapolis_Union_Station) is the state's primary [intercity bus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intercity_bus_service) transfer hub, served by seven carriers operating 12 routes.[^cite_note-483] [Amtrak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amtrak)'s *[Cardinal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cardinal_\(passenger_train\))* passenger rail service operates three weekly roundtrips between [New York City](645042.md) and [Chicago](6886.md). Located in the enclave of [Beech Grove](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beech_Grove,_Indiana), the [Beech Grove Shops](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beech_Grove_Shops) serve as Amtrak's primary heavy maintenance and overhaul facility.[^cite_note-Bogle-484] About 282 miles (454 km)[^cite_note-transport-466] of freight rail lines traverse the city, including one [Class I](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Railroad_classes#Class_I) railroad ([CSX Transportation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CSX_Transportation)), one [Class II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Railroad_classes#Class_II) railroad ([Indiana Rail Road](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indiana_Rail_Road)), and two [shortline railroads](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shortline_railroad) ([Indiana Southern Railroad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indiana_Southern_Railroad) and [Louisville and Indiana Railroad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Louisville_and_Indiana_Railroad)).

### Utilities



[Geist Reservoir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geist_Reservoir) in northeast Indianapolis is one of the region's four reservoirs.

[AES Indiana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AES_Indiana) supplies electricity to more than 500,000 Indianapolis customers[^cite_note-485] and maintains 90,000 street lights.[^cite_note-486] Natural gas, water, and wastewater utilities are provided by Citizens Energy Group.[^cite_note-487] The company's thermal division operates the [Perry K. Generating Station](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Perry_K._Generating_Station) which produces and distributes steam for heating and cooling to about 160 customers in downtown Indianapolis.[^cite_note-488] The city's water supply is sourced from the [White River](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_River_\(Indiana\)) and its tributaries as well as [aquifers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aquifer) via four surface water treatment plants, four reservoirs, and five groundwater pumping stations throughout the region.[^cite_note-489]

[Area codes 317 and 463](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Area_codes_317_and_463) are telephone [area codes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Area_code) in the [North American Numbering Plan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_American_Numbering_Plan) assigned to Indianapolis and seven surrounding counties in Central Indiana. Established in 1947, 317 is the original area code for the Indianapolis area, while 463 is an [overlay code](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Overlay_plan) for the same area that was added in 2016, making [ten-digit dialing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ten-digit_dialing) mandatory for all calls in the region.[^cite_note-490] Telecommunications, including cable television, internet, telephone, and wireless services, are provided by [AT\&T Communications](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AT\&T_Communications), Metronet, [Spectrum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spectrum_\(brand\)), [Verizon Communications](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Verizon_Communications), and [Xfinity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xfinity).[^cite_note-491]

[Waste collection](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waste_collection) services in Indianapolis are provided by the city's Department of Public Works Solid Waste Division, [Republic Services](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republic_Services), and [WM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waste_Management_\(corporation\)).[^cite_note-492] Solid waste disposal in the city is processed by [landfill](211285.md) and [incineration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Incineration). [Reworld](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reworld) operates a [waste-to-energy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waste-to-energy) plant in the city. About 11% of residents subscribe to private [curbside recycling](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Curbside_recycling) services;[^cite_note-Russell2023-493] however, free public recycling drop-off sites are available throughout the city.[^cite_note-494] Of U.S. cities, Indianapolis is the largest without a universal curbside recycling program, resulting in one of the lowest [landfill diversion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Landfill_diversion) rates.[^cite_note-Russell2023-493][^cite_note-495]

### Healthcare

See also: [List of hospitals in Indianapolis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_hospitals_in_Indianapolis)



[Sidney & Lois Eskenazi Hospital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sidney_&_Lois_Eskenazi_Hospital), the city's flagship [safety net hospital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Safety_net_hospital)

Healthcare in Indianapolis is provided by about 20 hospitals, most belonging to the [private](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Private_hospital), [non-profit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-profit_hospital) [hospital networks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hospital_network) of [Ascension](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ascension_\(healthcare_system\)) [St. Vincent Health](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/St._Vincent_Health), Community Health Network,[^cite_note-496] and [Indiana University Health](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indiana_University_Health).[^cite_note-497] Several are [teaching hospitals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teaching_hospitals) affiliated with the [IU School of Medicine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IU_School_of_Medicine) or Marian University's [Tom and Julie Wood College of Osteopathic Medicine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tom_and_Julie_Wood_College_of_Osteopathic_Medicine). Four hospitals are [Level I trauma centers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trauma_center).[^cite_note-498]

Health and Hospital Corporation of Marion County oversees the city's public health facilities and programs, including the Marion County Public Health Department, Indianapolis Emergency Medical Services, and Eskenazi Health.[^cite_note-499] Eskenazi Health operates ten [primary care](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Primary_care) sites across the city, including the [Sidney & Lois Eskenazi Hospital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sidney_&_Lois_Eskenazi_Hospital).[^cite_note-500] Other [public hospitals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_hospital) include the Richard L. Roudebush VA Medical Center (managed by the [Veterans Health Administration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Veterans_Health_Administration))[^cite_note-501] and the [NeuroDiagnostic Institute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NeuroDiagnostic_Institute) (managed by the State of Indiana).[^cite_note-502]

IU Health [Methodist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indiana_University_Health_Methodist_Hospital), [University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indiana_University_Health_University_Hospital), and [Riley Hospital for Children](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Riley_Hospital_for_Children) are affiliated with the IU School of Medicine. Riley is among the nation's foremost [children's hospitals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Children's_hospital), recognized in all ten pediatric specialties by *[U.S. News & World Report](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._News_&_World_Report)*.[^cite_note-503] IU Health is consolidating and replacing Methodist and University hospitals with a new \$4.3 billion [academic medical center](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academic_medical_center) which is slated to open in 2027.[^cite_note-Methodist-504] Other major hospitals include [Ascension St. Vincent Hospital - Indianapolis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/St._Vincent_Indianapolis_Hospital), [Community Hospital East](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Community_Hospital_East), [Community Hospital North](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Community_Hospital_North), and [Franciscan Health Indianapolis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franciscan_Health_Indianapolis).

## Sister cities

Indianapolis's [sister cities](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sister_city) are:[^cite_note-505][^cite_note-508]

- [Brazil](3383.md) [Campinas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Campinas), Brazil

- [Germany](11867.md) [Cologne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cologne), Germany

- [China](5405.md) [Hangzhou](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hangzhou), China

- [India](14533.md) [Hyderabad](37534.md), India

- [France](5843419.md) [Le Mans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Mans), France

- [Italy](14532.md) [Monza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Monza), Italy

- [England](9316.md) [Northamptonshire](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northamptonshire), England, United Kingdom

- [Nigeria](21383.md) [Onitsha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Onitsha), Nigeria

- [Slovenia](27338.md) [Piran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piran), Slovenia

- [Mexico](3966054.md) [Querétaro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quer%C3%A9taro_\(city\)), Mexico

- [Taiwan](25734.md) [Taipei](57648.md), Taiwan
